Update 2026-11-05 (b-i Try-Tiny `||` FIXED, branch `backlog/657-try-tiny-or`): the 5 Sub-Name extras reproduce on the one-atom cell `dev-perl/Try-Tiny` in a single pass (first-walk `||` choice, not a latch or gate). Cause, established by a Portage 3.0.83 ResolverPlayground oracle (scratch probe `probe_tt.py`, M4 dicts of `LLM/evidence/2026-11-03-647/probe647_oracle.py`): Portage builds ONE conjunction per selected virtual, not one per tree version. `_dep_check_composite_db.match_pkgs` (`depgraph.py:11964-12043`) returns the `_select_package` result (installed without `--update`, highest with it) plus extras only for distinct (slot, sub_slot); `_expand_new_virtuals` (`dep_check.py:220-246`) resolves that virtual's nested `||` with an inner `dep_check`/`dep_zapdeps` first, and the outer `||` ranks the flat conjunction as one choice. portuale expanded every tree version (1.700.0, 1.680.100_rc, 1.630.0) with the nested `||` flattened as an AND; the nonexistent `~perl-core/..-1.700.0` atoms ranked them `OtherInstalledSome` below `Sub-Name` (`Available`). Oracle outcomes: bare `Try-Tiny` keeps the virtual (`preferred_installed` beats `preferred_non_installed`); with `=perl-5.44.0` in argv the inner pick `=perl-5.42*` is an `installed_downgrade` (`dep_check.py:531-541`, `:634-635`), the conjunction drops to `other` and `Sub-Name` wins (M6b); with `--update` the 1.700.0 conjunction is expanded and the virtual upgrades. Fix: `expand_new_style_virtual_arm` takes the single-version resolved shape for the installed version whenever all tree versions share one (slot, sub_slot) (`shares_one_slot`); distinct slots keep the per-version loop. Pins: RED->GREEN `tests_p647::bare_consumer_keeps_the_installed_multi_version_virtual`; the M6b and -uD p647 tests stay green. Bed (9-atom L0 list, `l0-20261105T224442Z`): the Sub-Name extras of XML-Parser, kdecore-meta, texlive, texlive-core and texlive-latex are gone, Try-Tiny's merge list equals Portage's; what remains on those cells is the missing skip block (Portage schedules perl 5.44 and skips it, portuale does not schedule it) and the dev-lang_perl skip block parent list (10 rows vs 69). Skip-block origin TRACED (Portage `emerge -pv --debug dev-perl/Try-Tiny` in the bed, log `differential-test-bed/logs/l0-20261105T230556Z/real/dev-perl_Try-Tiny.txt`): in ONE first walk Portage (1) pops Try-Tiny's `|| ( >=virtual/perl-SLU-1.400.0 dev-perl/Sub-Name )` (log l.71-129) and keeps the installed virtual 1.680.100_rc with its `=perl-5.42*` arm; (2) then walks the installed virtuals the same ebuild pulls (`virtual/perl-Exporter`, `virtual/perl-Carp`, ...) and their RDEPEND `|| ( =dev-lang/perl-5.44* =dev-lang/perl-5.42* ... )` first arm schedules `dev-lang/perl-5.44.0` (l.166-207, `Parent Dep: =dev-lang/perl-5.44* required by (virtual/perl-Exporter..., installed)`); (3) 5.44 collides with the installed 5.42.2 that 68 virtual/perl-* holders pin: slot conflict (l.19698), `backtracking try 1`, `runtime_pkg_mask` on perl; (4) the final list is Try-Tiny alone plus the `skipped due to unsatisfied dependencies` block. So 5.44 enters AFTER the SLU choice, through the installed virtuals' `||` first arm, not through Try-Tiny's own `||`; portuale never walks those installed virtuals' alternatives on this cell, so it has no conflict, no backtrack and no skip block (that also explains the 10-vs-69 parent rows on dev-lang_perl). Skip-block mechanism and two failed fix attempts (2026-11-06): `_add_pkg_dep_string` (`depgraph.py` ~4540-4700) adds the atoms selected from a pulled new-style virtual's inline expansion (`selected_atoms`/`traversed_virt_pkgs`) whatever the depth, and the virtual's inner `dep_zapdeps` takes the HIGHER same-cp arm ("prefer upgrades", `dep_check.py:796-797`; `all_installed_slots` ignores the sub-slot), so `virtual/perl-Exporter` schedules perl-5.44.0, a sibling virtual pinning `=perl-5.42*` makes a slot conflict and the backtrack masks 5.44 (oracle P1mm). Pinned by the ignored RED `tests_p647::installed_virtual_with_a_higher_perl_arm_schedules_then_skips_the_transient` (fixtures pmtest `e9ea403`). portuale walks an installed child only when `deep.recurses_at(depth)` (`pass/unresolved_dep.rs`, `enqueue_installed_deps`). Attempt 1 (walk every installed virtual): RED goes green, 13 oracle-pinned tests break (p650/p651/p652b/p656 family, p572, `graph::update_without_deep_keeps_an_installed_virtual_provider_silent`). Attempt 2 (only virtuals pulled by a merge-bound puller AND with a tree ebuild of the installed version): the libc and one p572 test recover, 11 still break (p650, p651, p652b, five p656, p572 q19/onlydeps, consumer_or_view sibling). Diagnosis: Portage decides the virtual's inner `||` DURING the puller's expansion, before the transient is in the graph (pre-demoter judgment), whereas portuale's installed-walk defers `||` groups to the disjunctive stack, which sees 5.44 already scheduled and falls through to the enccore/thcore arm. A correct fix needs the inline decision at enqueue time (state of the puller's expansion), not a pop-time walk; the posture/seed machinery (`rev_dep_or.rs`) models exactly that timing for the cases it covers. Not shipped.

659. **DONE 2026-11-06 (test-only change in `crates/portuale/src/embedded_runtime.rs`, no pmtest counterpart; the other `remote::tests` with the local transport already pass and do not read the host's `/etc/portage`) — was: OPEN (filed 2026-11-06, found while closing #451) — `embedded_runtime::tests::a_phase_running_merge_works_from_an_extracted_runtime` depends on the host's `/etc/portage`.** The test runs `mrg --remote-transport=local` with `--remote-root` under a temp dir, but `pull_dir` (`crates/portuale/src/remote/ssh.rs`) runs `tar -c -C /etc/portage .` on the host itself. Here `/etc/portage` holds a root-only `gnupg.getuto.tmp/`, so `tar` exits 2 as a normal user and the test fails with `mrg: pulling /etc/portage from localtest failed (exit 2)` (also outside the sandbox). Fix: make the local-transport pull read the fixtures' config root (or have the test point the pull at a fixture directory) so the result no longer depends on the machine's `/etc/portage`; check the other `remote::tests` that use `--remote-transport=local` for the same dependency. [A] **Fixed:** the test builds its own client config under its temp dir (absolute `repos.conf` locations for `testrepo` and `overlay`, `make.conf`, an absolute `make.profile` link) and passes `--remote-etc-portage=client:<that dir>`; the fixture `repos.conf` could not be pulled as is because its locations are relative to the config root. Verified: fmt, clippy zero warnings, `cargo test --release --workspace` 32 suites, 0 failures.
679. **WITHDRAWN 2026-11-07 (charter sweep: no wrong package, order or misleading message; only a difference from Portage, charter says not to chase it) — was OPEN (filed 2026-11-07, found while settling #672) — `installed in '<ROOT>'` / `ebuild scheduled for merge to '<ROOT>'` keep the ROOT as given; Portage normalises it with a trailing slash.** With `ROOT=/x/r` Portage prints `installed in '/x/r/'`, portuale `installed in '/x/r'` (`installed_in_root`, `pretend/problem_reports.rs`); with `ROOT=/x/r/` both agree (captured with the #605 `w605conb` shape, `=dev-libs/w605x-2`, `-pv`). The fixture pins use the slash-less ROOT, so a fix moves them. Tier 3.
